# Keycloak Operator

[![Artifact Hub](https://img.shields.io/endpoint?url=https://artifacthub.io/badge/repository/keycloak-operator)](https://artifacthub.io/packages/search?repo=keycloak-operator)

Helm chart for rendering Keycloak Operator custom resources from declarative values.

The chart does not install the Keycloak operator deployment or its CRDs. It renders the supported custom resources expected to already exist in the target cluster.

The values contract and local smoke schemas are aligned with [keycloak/keycloak-k8s-resources 26.6.1](https://github.com/keycloak/keycloak-k8s-resources/tree/26.6.1/kubernetes), specifically the storage-version CRDs for `Keycloak` and `KeycloakRealmImport` in `k8s.keycloak.org/v2beta1`.

## Quick Start

Install the chart:

```bash
helm install keycloak-operator oci://ghcr.io/riftonix/helm-shared/libs/keycloak-operator   --namespace keycloak   --create-namespace
```

Install the local README generator hook:

```bash
pre-commit install
pre-commit install-hooks
```

## Supported Resources

- `Keycloak`
- `KeycloakRealmImport`

## Values Model

The chart exposes one keyed map per supported resource kind:

- `keycloaks`
- `keycloakRealmImports`

Each map follows the same generic contract used by dependency charts for `appchart`: the key becomes `metadata.name`, while `namespace`, `labels`, `annotations`, `apiVersion`, `spec`, and `status` remain configurable per resource.

`values.yaml` intentionally contains documentation placeholder objects under `__helm_docs_example__` so `helm-docs` can emit nested documentation for upstream `spec` fields instead of only the top-level chart keys.

Global controls:

- `nameOverride`
- `releasePrefix`
- `commonLabels`
- `commonAnnotations`
- `global`
- `apiVersions.*`

The values contract is validated by [values.schema.json](values.schema.json).

## Included Values Files

- [values.yaml](values.yaml): minimal defaults that render no resources.
- [values.yaml.example](values.yaml.example): representative examples for both supported resource kinds.

## Testing

Representative local commands:

```bash
helm lint . -f values.yaml.example
helm template keycloak-operator . -f values.yaml.example
helm unittest -f 'tests/units/*_test.yaml' .
sh tests/units/backward_compatibility_test.sh
python3 tests/smokes/run/smoke.py
make test-smoke-fast
make test-e2e
```
